Abstract: Despite recent progress, LLM agents still struggle with reasoning over long interaction histories. While current memory-augmented agents rely on a static retrieve-then-reason paradigm, this rigid pipeline design prevents them from dynamically adapting memory access to intermediate evidence discovered during inference. To bridge this gap, we propose MRAgent, a framework that combines an associative memory graph with an active reconstruction mechanism. We represent memory as a Cue-Tag-Content graph, where associative tags serve as semantic bridges connecting fine-grained cues to memory contents. Operating on this structure, our active reconstruction mechanism integrates LLM reasoning directly into memory access, allowing the agent to iteratively explore and prune retrieval paths based on accumulated evidence. This ensures that memory retrieval is dynamically adapted to the reasoning context while avoiding combinatorial explosion caused by unconstrained expansion. Experiments on the LoCoMo benchmark and LongMemEval benchmark demonstrate significant improvements over strong baselines (up to 23%), while substantially reducing token and runtime cost, highlighting the effectiveness of active and associative reconstruction for long-horizon memory reasoning.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Similarity: Trustworthy Memory Search for Personal AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06054)
*Jiawen Zhang,Kejia Chen,Jiachen Ma,Yangfan Hu,Lipeng He,Yechao Zhang,Jian Liu,Xiaohu Yang,Tianwei Zhang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对个人AI代理长期记忆的信任问题，提出MemGate插件，通过查询条件化的神经门控机制来控制记忆检索，减少跨领域泄露、迎合、工具调用漂移和记忆诱导越狱等威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人AI代理的长期记忆管道主要依赖语义相似性检索，这导致语义相关但上下文不合适的记忆被注入模型，引发跨领域泄露、迎合、工具调用漂移或记忆诱导越狱等信任问题。

Method: 评估A-Mem、Mem0、MemOS等代表性代理记忆框架，以及具有持久状态和工具使用能力的真实环境OpenClaw。提出MemGate作为轻量级记忆插件（9M参数，35.1MB），插入向量存储和LLM骨干之间，使用查询条件化神经门控将原始相似性搜索转化为任务条件化记忆准入。

Result: 长期记忆不仅是实用层，而是可重塑代理任务解释和动作执行的控制通道，使其高度易受上述威胁。MemGate在多种主流记忆框架、真实代理设置和不同LLM骨干上减少了记忆诱导威胁，同时保留了长期记忆效用。

Conclusion: 长期记忆是个人AI代理中的信任边界，应被谨慎管理。MemGate提供了一种无需修改LLM、记忆数据库重写或推理时LLM判断的部署方案，在保持记忆功能的同时显著降低了相关安全风险。

Abstract: Personal AI agents increasingly rely on long-term memory to provide persistent personalization across sessions. However, existing memory pipelines are largely driven by semantic similarity: memory data close to the current query is retrieved and injected into the model context. This creates a critical trustworthiness gap, since a semantically related memory may still be contextually inappropriate, leading to threats such as cross-domain leakage, sycophancy, tool-call drift, or memory-induced jailbreaks.
  In this paper, we study memory search as a trust boundary in personal AI agents. We evaluate representative agentic memory frameworks, including A-Mem, Mem0, and MemOS, together with OpenClaw, a real-world personal-agent environment with persistent state and tool-use capability. Our results show that long-term memory is not merely a utility layer, but a durable control channel that can reshape how agents interpret tasks and execute actions, leaving them highly susceptible to the aforementioned threats. To mitigate these vulnerabilities, we propose MemGate, a lightweight and deployable memory plug-in for trustworthy memory search, with only 9M parameters and a 35.1MB footprint. MemGate is inserted between the vector memory store and the backbone LLM, requiring no LLM modification, memory-database rewriting, or inference-time LLM judge. It applies a query-conditioned neural gate to candidate memory representations, turning raw similarity search into task-conditioned memory admission. Across multiple mainstream memory frameworks, real-world agent settings, and diverse LLM backbones, MemGate reduces memory-induced threats while preserving long-term memory utility.

</details>


### [113] [When Should Memory Stay Silent: Measuring Memory-Use Boundaries in Memory-Augmented Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06055)
*Lingxiang Xu,Jiaoyun Yang,Min Hu,Hongtu Chen,Ning An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RBI-Eval是一个控制测量研究，通过探针集比较模型在有无敏感记忆访问时的行为差异，发现模型在敏感记忆存在时会对响应产生显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使语言模型能够支持个性化交互，但检索到的敏感记忆内容是否值得在当前轮次中使用尚不明确。现有记忆评估方法侧重检索准确性和下游任务效用，忽略了检索到的敏感记忆内容是否值得在当前轮次中使用。

Method: RBI-Eval围绕探针集构建控制测量研究，比较模型在有/无敏感记忆访问时的行为。评估四个基础LLM与匹配无记忆参考，在四种记忆访问设置下：全上下文暴露和三个检索系统。

Result: 研究发现显著的行为差异：GPT-5.4-mini的敏感记忆分离分数降低8.9%-26.6%，Claude-Sonnet-4.6、DeepSeek-V4-Flash、Qwen3.5-9B降低51.1%-82.9%。控制实验表明效果仅针对敏感内容而非一般个性化。检索系统减少暴露但无法消除生成器处的记忆整合。

Conclusion: 安全个性化需要在检索和生成阶段都做出记忆感知的决策。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language model agents to support personalized interactions, but it remains unclear when available memories warrant integration into responses. Existing memory evaluations emphasize retrieval accuracy and downstream task utility, while overlooking whether retrieved sensitive memory content is warranted in the current turn. We introduce RBI-Eval, a controlled measurement study built around a probe set that compares model behavior with and without access to sensitive memory under identical benign prompts. We evaluate four base LLMs against a matched no-memory reference across four memory-access settings: full-context exposure and three retrieval systems. Our results reveal substantial behavioral divergence. With memory available, the separation score for sensitive-memory integration decreases by 8.9\%--26.6\% relative to the matched no-memory reference for GPT-5.4-mini, but by 51.1\%--82.9\% for Claude-Sonnet-4.6, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and Qwen3.5-9B. Control experiments on DeepSeek and GPT-5.4-mini show this effect is specific to sensitive content, rather than general personalization. Retrieval systems reduce exposure but do not eliminate integration once sensitive memory reaches the generator. These findings suggest safe personalization requires memory-aware decisions at both retrieval and generation time.

</details>


### [114] [Towards Healthy Evolution: Exploring the Role and Mechanisms of Human-Agent Interaction in Self-Evolving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06114)
*Dianxing Shi,Junqi He,Junhao Chen,Bowen Wang,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ANCHOR框架，通过模拟人类监督在自我进化智能体系统中提供反馈，验证了有限监督能有效缓解安全退化同时保持性能稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化智能体通过持续自我对弈改进，但自主进化可能导致能力退化和安全漂移；人类反馈在静态智能体中有效，但在自我进化系统中的作用未被充分探索。

Method: 引入ANCHOR（Agent Norm Correction through Human-like Oversight and Review）框架，基于LLM模拟人类监督并在自我进化各阶段提供反馈，在编码、数学推理和安全三个领域评估两个代表性开源自我进化智能体系统。

Result: 有限监督显著缓解安全退化，同时保持核心进化目标的稳定性能；输出验证阶段的监督最有效，增加监督频率的回报递减。

Conclusion: 为设计更稳定、可控和人类对齐的自我进化智能体系统提供了实证证据和实践指导，表明适当的人类监督可以平衡进化效果与安全性。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve through continual self-play and self-generated learning signals, but autonomous evolution can also cause capability degradation and safety drift. Although human feedback has proven effective for static and post-trained agents, its role in self-evolving systems remains underexplored. We introduce Agent Norm Correction through Human-like Oversight and Review (ANCHOR), an LLM-based framework that simulates human supervision and delivers feedback at various phases of self-evolution. With ANCHOR, we evaluate two representative open-source self-evolving agent systems across coding, mathematical reasoning, and safety. Our results show that even limited supervision substantially mitigates safety degradation while preserving stable performance on core evolutionary objectives. Further analysis shows that supervision over the output verification phase is the most effective for intervention, whereas increasing supervision frequency yields diminishing returns. These findings provide empirical evidence and practical guidance for designing more stable, controllable, and human-aligned self-evolving agent systems.

</details>


### [115] [From Reward-Hack Activations to Agentic Risk States: Context-Calibrated Mechanistic Monitoring in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06223)
*Patrick Wilhelm,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究针对语言模型代理中的奖励黑客行为，提出了一种基于激活动力学、熵和决策上下文的综合安全监控方法。研究发现，适配器微调可将奖励黑客倾向转移至代理行动选择中，但仅靠激活动力学无法有效缓解此类行为；高奖励黑客激活仅识别潜在策略状态，不一定导致立即攻击；熵和上下文校准的内部特征能更好地估计风险，激活方向引导进一步减少了代理行为中的代理攻击行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理通过观察、推理和行动选择循环工作，其安全监控依赖于内部模型状态和环境上下文。现有方法主要关注激活动力学，但可能不足以检测奖励黑客行为，特别是在环境暴露代理奖励 affordances 时。需要一种更全面的方法来识别和缓解代理中的奖励黑客倾向。

Method: 在ReAct风格的代理框架下研究奖励黑客监控，使用Gameable ALFWorld和WebShop作为测试环境。采用三种特征：基于激活的奖励黑客分数、token级熵和决策上下文特征。在School-of-Reward-Hacks数据集上微调适配器以转移奖励黑客倾向。通过激活方向引导技术进一步减少代理行为中的代理攻击行为。

Result: 1) 适配器微调可将奖励黑客倾向转移至代理行动选择，特别是在环境暴露代理奖励 affordances 时；2) 单纯依赖激活动力学无法有效缓解此类行为；3) 高奖励黑客激活识别出潜在策略状态，但不一定导致立即攻击；4) 在下一步预测任务中，熵和上下文校准的内部特征比单独的奖励黑客激活能更好地估计风险；5) 激活方向引导进一步减少了代理行为中的代理攻击行为。

Conclusion: 研究支持为代理采用上下文校准的内部监控：奖励黑客激活识别潜在策略状态，而熵和决策上下文有助于确定该状态何时变得具有风险。这种组合方法比单独使用激活动力学更有效，为构建更安全的语言模型代理提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Language-model agents act through repeated cycles of observation, reasoning, and action selection, making safety monitoring depend on both internal model state and environment context. We study reward-hacking monitors in ReAct-style agents acting in Gameable ALFWorld and WebShop. Agents are instrumented with activation-based reward-hack scores, token-level entropy, and decision-context features. We find that adapters fine-tuned on \textit{School-of-Reward-Hacks} dataset can transfer reward-hack tendencies into agentic action selection, especially when the environment exposes proxy-reward affordances. However, mitigating such behavior cannot rely on activation dynamics alone. High reward-hack activation identifies a latent policy state, but does not necessarily imply an immediate exploit action. Across next-step prediction tasks, entropy and context-calibrated internal features improve risk estimation over reward-hack activation alone. Activation-direction steering further reduces proxy-exploit behavior in selected mixed-adapter regimes. Overall, our results support context-calibrated internal monitoring for agents: reward-hack activation identifies a latent policy state, while entropy and decision context help determine when that state becomes risky action.

</details>


### [116] [TokenMizer: Graph-Structured Session Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06337)
*Shweta Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TokenMizer是一个开源代理系统，将LLM会话历史建模为类型化知识图谱，通过混合提取管道和三层检查点系统，将上下文窗口扩展到MECW之外，同时保持结构化信息的完整性和可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM部署在长时任务中面临上下文窗口有限而工作会话无限的约束。当历史记录超过MECW时，关键的结构化信息（架构决策、任务转换、文件历史）会被静默丢弃。现有的缓解措施将历史视为平面文本，破坏了使会话可恢复的关系结构。

Method: TokenMizer将LLM会话历史建模为类型化知识图谱（14种节点类型，7种边类型）。采用混合提取管道增量填充图谱，三层检查点系统将其序列化为紧凑的恢复块。包含8层压缩管道（语义缓存）减少上下文开销，模糊标签匹配提升召回率。

Result: 在21个跨5个域的会话基准测试中，TokenMizer恢复块平均78个token（比基线小2倍），任务召回率51.0%，决策召回率46.6%，文件召回率58.7%。模糊标签匹配提升33个百分点，压缩实现47.3%的token减少且无需外部依赖。

Conclusion: TokenMizer提供了文本保留基线的可查询替代方案，具有一半的token成本，关键优势是保留了解决方案背后的推理过程而非仅技术提及，显著提升了会话恢复能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) deployments for long-horizon tasks face a fundamental constraint: context windows are finite while productive work sessions are not. When history exceeds the Maximum Effective Context Window (MECW), critical structured information - architectural decisions, task transitions, file histories - is silently discarded. Existing mitigations treat history as flat text, destroying the relational structure that makes sessions resumable. We present TokenMizer, an open-source proxy system that models LLM session history as a typed knowledge graph. The schema defines 14 node types and 7 edge types. A hybrid extraction pipeline populates the graph incrementally, while a three-tier checkpoint system serializes it into compact resume blocks. An 8-layer compression pipeline reduces context overhead, and a semantic cache reduces repeated-query latency. Evaluated on a controlled benchmark of 21 sessions spanning 5 domains, TokenMizer demonstrates significant token economy. It produces resume blocks averaging 78 tokens (range: 42-124) - 2x smaller than evaluated baselines (159-170 tokens) - while achieving higher decision recall (+9-17 percentage points). Crucially, baselines only preserve that a technology was mentioned; TokenMizer preserves the rationale. Across all sessions, TokenMizer achieves mean task recall 51.0%, decision recall 46.6%, and file recall 58.7%. Variance reflects domain heterogeneity: explicit imperative phrasing (software engineering) scores higher than implicit reasoning (research). Ablation studies show fuzzy label matching is the dominant improvement factor (+33 pp task recall). The heuristic compression achieves 47.3% token reduction with zero external dependencies. TokenMizer provides a queryable alternative to text-retention baselines at half the token cost.

</details>


### [117] [An Infectious Disease Spread Simulation Based on Large Language Model Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06360)
*Yonchanok Khaokaew,Ruochen Kong,Andreas Zufle,Hao Xue,Taylor Anderson,Chandini Raina MacIntyre,Matthew Scotch,Flora D. Salim,David J Heslop*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个基于大型语言模型的时空代理模拟框架，将LLM生成的流感样症状报告决策与人口普查数据结合，用于模拟传染病爆发期间的个体决策行为，捕捉社会和地理异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病爆发期间的个体决策建模对于理解行为动态和制定公共卫生干预措施至关重要。先前研究表明大型语言模型可以通过基于人口统计提示和情境上下文的代理决策来模拟现实人类行为，但需要更精细的时空建模来捕捉地理异质性。

Method: 研究构建了一个基于空间的代理模拟框架，包括：1) 集成LLM生成的流感样症状报告决策；2) 基于人口普查数据的合成人口代理；3) 将代理分配到城市空间单元中；4) 实现三种决策场景：独立推理、家庭影响和信息框架；5) 在旧金山和亚特兰大进行模拟。

Result: 研究发现收入和教育是报告率变化的主要驱动因素，地理、LLM模型选择和信息框架产生较小但一致的效应。该框架生成的合成数据同时捕捉了社会和地理异质性。

Conclusion: 该框架支持空间流行病学建模和偏差感知的行为分析，能够生成包含社会和地理异质性的合成数据，为理解传染病爆发期间的行为动态提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Modelling individual decision-making during infectious disease outbreaks is crucial for understanding behavioural dynamics and informing effective public health interventions. Prior work has shown that large language models can simulate realistic human behaviour by generating agent decisions based on demographic prompts and situational context. We build on this foundation with a spatially grounded, agent-based simulation framework that integrates LLM-generated decisions about self-reported influenza-like illness into a census-based synthetic population of agents. Location is treated as a central feature: agents are assigned to spatial units within cities, capturing the spatial distributions of different demographic groups using real-world census data and enabling geographically diverse behavioural modelling. We implement and compare three decision scenarios, independent reasoning, household influence, and message framing, and simulate self-reporting outcomes in San Francisco and Atlanta. Results reveal that income and education are the dominant drivers of reporting rate variation, with smaller but consistent effects from geography, LLM model choice, and message framing. Our framework generates synthetic data that captures both social and geographic heterogeneity, supporting spatial epidemiological modelling and bias-aware behavioural analysis.

</details>


### [118] [Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06388)
*Jiaju Chen,Yuxuan Lu,Jiayi Su,Chaoran Chen,Songlin Xiao,Zheng Zhang,Yun Wang,Yunyao Li,Jian Zhao,Tongshuang Wu,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ALMANAC是一个包含2,987个协作动作及其理论驱动的心理模型标注的数据集，源自社会学的经典配对路由任务（Map Task），记录参与者的自我推理、感知的伙伴意图和感知的团队目标，用于评估大型语言模型在模拟人类协作行为和推断潜在心理模型方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）代理已具备复杂认知能力，但缺乏在协作过程中持续维护和对齐心理模型的能力，而现有数据集缺少带有动作级心理模型标注的真实人类协作数据，因此需要构建此类数据集来指导代理向过程级协作能力发展。

Method: ALMANAC基于社会学的经典配对路由任务构建，包含2,987个协作动作，每个动作均配对理论驱动的心理模型标注，记录参与者的自我推理、感知的伙伴意图和感知的团队目标；同时基准测试了六个LLM在预测人类下一轮行为和心理模型方面的表现。

Result: 在ALMANAC上的基准测试表明，该数据集能有效评估模型模拟人类协作行为和推断其潜在心理模型的能力，为改进协作型智能体提供了新的评估工具。

Conclusion: ALMANAC通过提供带有动作级心理模型标注的真实人类协作数据，填补了当前社区在过程级协作能力训练与评估方面的空白，有助于推动更自然、有效的AI人类协作系统的发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents have enabled complex cognitive capabilities, such as multi-step reasoning, planning, and tool use, that increasingly position these agents as human collaborators. Effective collaboration, however, requires collaborators to continuously maintain and align mental models of their own reasoning,partners' intentions, and shared goals during the collaborative process. Today's agents rarely develop such capabilities since they are primarily optimized for task completion, and the community lacks authentic human collaboration data with action-level mental model annotations that could guide agents toward process-level collaborative competence. To bridge this gap, we present ALMANAC, a dataset of Action-Level Mental model ANnotations for Agent Collaboration built from the Map Task, a classic dyadic routing task from social science. ALMANAC contains 2,987 collaboration actions, each paired with theory-informed mental model annotations that record the participants' self-reasoning, perceived partner intent, and perceived team goal. We benchmark six LLMs on predicting humans' next-turn behavior and mental models. Our results demonstrate ALMANAC's utility in evaluating models' ability to simulate human collaborative behaviors and infer their underlying mental models.

</details>


### [119] [Risk Assessment of Autonomous Driving: Integrating Technical Failures, Ethical Dilemmas, and Policy Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06396)
*Boyi Chen,Shengqin Chu,Zicheng Wang,Brian Baetz,Zhen Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析碰撞数据、脱节报告、道德机器数据集和法规比较，评估自动驾驶的技术风险、伦理框架和监管差异，并提出工程标准、伦理讨论与制度监督相结合的协同治理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶技术有潜力减少人为错误导致的交通事故，但也带来新的风险，需要从技术、伦理和法规三个维度进行系统评估。

Method: 基于美国国家公路交通安全管理局（NHTSA）的公开碰撞数据、加利福尼亚州机动车管理局（DMV）的脱节报告、MIT道德机器数据集，以及对五个司法管辖区的比较法规分析，综合评估自动驾驶的技术故障模式、伦理框架和监管环境。

Result: 主要技术故障模式为感知和分类错误，在报告的事故中占相当大比例；自动驾驶车辆决策存在不同的伦理框架，不同地区的法规不一致增加了广泛应用的确定性。

Conclusion: 技术、伦理和法规问题密切相关，需要共同解决。建议采用一种结合工程标准、伦理讨论和制度监督的更具适应性和合作性的治理方法。

Abstract: Autonomous driving technology has the potential to reduce the large number of road traffic accidents caused by human error each year, but it also brings new types of risks that need to be evaluated from the aspects of technology, ethics and regulations. Based on public crash data from the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration (NHTSA), disengagement reports from the California Department of Motor Vehicles (DMV), the MIT Moral Machines dataset, and a comparative regulatory analysis of five jurisdictions, we have found that the main types of technical failure modes are perception and classification errors. These account for a relatively large proportion of the reported accidents, and it can be concluded that there are different ethical frameworks for autonomous vehicle decision-making, and inconsistent regulations in different areas increase the uncertainty of widespread application. Generally speaking, the problems of technology, ethics and regulation are closely related and need to be solved together. Therefore, this paper recommends a more adaptive and cooperative governance approach that combines engineering standards, ethical discussion, and institutional supervision.

</details>


### [120] [Agent Memory: Characterization and System Implications of Stateful Long-Horizon Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06448)
*Yasmine Omri,Ziyu Gan,Zachary Broveak,Robin Geens,Zexue He,Alex Pentland,Marian Verhelst,Tsachy Weissman,Thierry Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM智能体的记忆系统进行了系统级特性刻画，提出了四轴分类法、成本分析框架以及10项系统级优化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在处理需要长期推理和扩展交互历史的任务时，需要在跨会话中持久存储、检索和更新自身记忆。然而，这些记忆系统的系统级行为特征尚未被充分认识。

Method: 1. 引入基于系统的四轴分类法对智能体记忆系统进行分类；2. 构建了阶段感知的性能分析框架，将成本分解为构建、检索和生成三个阶段；3. 在两个基准测试套件上分析了10个代表性系统。

Result: 揭示了设计选择如何影响写入和读取路径的成本分布；推导出10项系统级建议，涵盖构建调度、能力下限、查询量摊销、新鲜度-延迟权衡以及集群规模管理。

Conclusion: 本文是首个针对智能体记忆系统的系统性研究，为大规模部署提供了理论基础和实践指导，平衡了性能开销与功能需求。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed on long-horizon tasks requiring sustained reasoning over extended interaction histories. Realizing this at scale requires agents to persistently store, retrieve, and update their own memory across sessions. A rich ecosystem of agent memory systems has emerged spanning flat retrieval, LLM-mediated extraction, consolidating fact stores, and agentic control flows. Yet, their system-level behavior remains uncharacterized. We present the first systems characterization of agent memory. First, we introduce a system-oriented taxonomy classifying agent memory systems along four axes. Second, we build a phase-aware profiling harness attributing cost to construction, retrieval, and generation. Third, we characterize ten representative systems across two benchmark suites, uncovering how design choices shift cost across the write and read paths. Finally, we derive 10 system recommendations covering construction scheduling, capability floors, amortization via query volume, freshness-latency tradeoffs, and fleet-scale management.

</details>


### [121] [Vortex: Efficient and Programmable Sparse Attention Serving for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06453)
*Zhuoming Chen,Xinrui Zhong,Qilong Feng,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Yang Zhou,Michael Qizhe Shieh,Zhihao Jia,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vortex是一个用于快速原型开发、部署和评估稀疏注意力算法的系统，它通过结合Python嵌入式前端语言和页面中心张量抽象，以及与LLM服务栈紧密集成的高效后端，实现了从理论效率到实际吞吐量提升的转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成长度的增长，稀疏注意力变得越来越重要，但部署和评估稀疏注意力算法在规模化时仍然高度工程密集型，严重阻碍了人类研究人员和AI代理对稀疏注意力设计的探索。

Method: Vortex系统包含两部分：1）Python嵌入式前端语言结合页面中心张量抽象，用于表达广泛的稀疏注意力算法；2）高效后端与现代LLM服务栈紧密集成。该系统支持快速原型开发、部署和评估稀疏注意力算法。

Result: AI代理使用Vortex自动生成和优化算法，最佳结果达到比全注意力高3.46倍吞吐量且保持准确性；在MLA架构的GLM-4.7-Flash上达到4.7倍吞吐量提升，在229B参数的MiniMax-M2.7上达到1.37倍提升（NVIDIA B200 GPU）。

Conclusion: Vortex显著加速了稀疏注意力算法的设计和迭代，成功将理论效率增益转化为实际吞吐量改进，并能扩展到新兴架构和超大模型。

Abstract: Sparse attention is becoming increasingly important for serving large language models (LLMs) as generation lengths continue to grow. However, deploying and evaluating new sparse attention algorithms at scale remains highly engineering-intensive, slowing both human researchers and AI agents in exploring the sparse attention design. To address this challenge, we present Vortex, a system that combines a Python-embedded frontend language atop a page-centric tensor abstraction for expressing a broad range of sparse attention algorithms, with an efficient backend tightly integrated into modern LLM serving stacks. Vortex enables rapid prototyping, deployment, and evaluation of sparse attention algorithms, effectively translating their theoretical efficiency gains into real-world throughput improvements. As a result, Vortex substantially accelerates the design and iteration of sparse attention algorithms. First, AI agents use Vortex to automatically generate and refine diverse algorithms, the best reaching up to $3.46\times$ higher throughput than full attention while preserving accuracy. Second, Vortex extends sparse attention to emerging architectures and very large models that are otherwise hard to experiment with, reaching up to $4.7\times$ higher throughput on the MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash and $1.37\times$ on the 229B-parameter MiniMax-M2.7 on NVIDIA B200 GPUs.

</details>


### [122] [Benchmark Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06462)
*Shiyun Xiong,Dongming Wu,Peiwen Sun,Yuang Ai,Bokang Yang,Wencheng Han,Xiao-Hui Li,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Benchmark Agent是一个全自动的智能体系统，用于构建LLM和MLLM的评估基准。该系统通过编排从查询分析到数据标注的全流程，高效生成多样化的高质量基准测试样本，解决了现有基准构建耗时费力、难复用以及性能饱和等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试存在三个主要问题：1）构建过程劳动密集且难以复用，影响可持续性和可扩展性；2）基准发布后性能迅速饱和，导致对最先进模型的区分度不足；3）缺乏能快速迭代更新的基准来推动研究进展。

Method: Benchmark Agent是一个完全自主的智能体系统，编排基准构建全流程：用户查询分析、子任务设计、数据标注和质量控制。系统被实现为生成15个代表性行业基准，覆盖文本理解、多模态理解和领域特定推理等多样化评估场景。评估方法包括人工评估、LLM-as-a-judge评估和一致性检查。

Result: 实验表明Benchmark Agent能够在极小人工干预下生成高质量基准样本。持续评估发现当前模型在特定领域推理任务上存在困难。基准发布后性能饱和现象得到缓解，系统能够持续生成具有区分度的测试数据。

Conclusion: 快速演进的基准测试可以显著促进研究社区发展，Benchmark Agent为解决基准构建中的可持续性和可扩展性挑战提供了可行方案。预览和代码将在演示页面和代码库中公开。

Abstract: Benchmarks are fundamental for evaluating and advancing LLMs and MLLMs by providing standardized and explicit measures of performance. However, their construction is labor-intensive and hard to reuse, raising concerns about sustainability and scalability. Moreover, existing benchmarks often quickly reach performance saturation after their release, resulting in insufficient discrimination among state-of-the-art models. To address these challenges, we introduce Benchmark Agent, a fully autonomous agentic system designed for benchmark building. Our framework orchestrates the complete benchmark construction pipeline, from user query analysis and subtask design to data annotation and quality control. To assess Benchmark Agent, we implement it to produce 15 representative benchmarks, spanning diverse evaluation scenarios, including text understanding, multimodal understanding, and domain-specific reasoning. Extensive experiments, including human evaluation, LLM-as-a-judge assessment, and consistency checks, demonstrate Benchmark Agent can generate high-quality benchmark samples with minimal human involvement. More importantly, through continual evaluation, we observe several insightful findings, including that current models struggle with certain domain-specific reasoning tasks. We believe that rapidly evolving benchmarks can contribute significantly to the research community. The preview and code will be publicly available at the demo page and code repository.

</details>


### [123] [MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
*Shangheng Du,Xiangchao Yan,Jinxin Shi,Zongsheng Cao,Shiyang Feng,Zichen Liang,Boyuan Sun,Tianshuo Peng,Yifan Zhou,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLEvolve是一个基于LLM的自进化多智能体框架，用于端到端的机器学习算法发现，通过渐进式树搜索、回顾性记忆和自适应编码模式解决了长周期优化中的分支信息隔离、无记忆搜索和缺乏层级控制等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLE智能体存在分支间信息隔离、无记忆搜索和缺乏层级控制三大问题，共同阻碍了长周期优化任务的完成。特别是在科学发现和机器学习工程等需要持续自我进化的任务中，这些限制严重影响了智能体的性能和进化能力。

Method: 1. 扩展树搜索为渐进式MCGS，通过基于图的参考边实现跨分支信息流；2. 引入回顾性记忆，结合冷启动领域知识库和动态全局记忆，支持任务特定经验的检索和复用；3. 将战略规划与代码生成解耦，采用自适应编码模式；4. 使用基于熵的渐进式调度，使搜索从广泛探索转向专注利用。

Result: 在MLE-Bench基准测试中，MLEvolve在12小时预算下（标准运行时间的一半）实现了平均奖牌率和有效提交率的最先进性能。在数学算法优化任务上，MLEvolve超越了包括AlphaEvolve在内的专用算法发现方法，展现出强大的跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLEvolve通过创新的信息流机制、记忆系统和自适应规划策略，有效解决了长周期机器学习算法发现中的关键挑战，证明了LLM智能体在持续自我进化方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [OLIVE: Online Low-Rank Incremental Learning for Efficient Adaptive Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05234)
*Dong Liu,Yanxuan Yu,Ben Lengerich,Tony Geng,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OLIVE是一种在线低秩增量学习框架，通过将自适应控制策略分解为低秩残差形式（W = A B^T），在保持预训练基控制器稳定性的同时，将在线更新成本从O(dk)降低到O(r(d+k))，仅依赖身体传感器反馈实现参数更新，通过门控机制和动态秩调度器实现跨地形（平坦路面、楼梯、坡道、不平整地形）的个性化适应，在7.4ms延迟下1800步内收敛，在步态平滑性、努力减少和运动稳定性方面分别比最强基线提升13%、22%和15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴外骨骼控制器依赖于静态步态策略，缺乏对动态真实环境和个体用户特征的适应能力，限制了其在复杂场景中的性能表现。

Method: 1. 采用低秩残差形式分解自适应组件：W = A B^T，其中秩r≪min(d,k)；2. 仅使用身体传感器反馈（肌电信号EMG、惯性测量单元IMU、振动传感器）进行奖励 shaped 策略梯度参数更新；3. 引入门控机制根据上下文状态调节个性化强度；4. 设计动态秩调度器，根据地形复杂度调整更新维度（简单地形分配最小容量，复杂地形扩展高秩更新）。

Result: 在可穿戴平台上实现：1）相比最强基线，步态平滑性、努力减少、运动稳定性分别提升13、22、15个百分点；2）在约1800步行步内收敛；3）端到端延迟7.4ms。

Conclusion: OLIVE能够在实时约束下实现跨多种活动（平坦行走、楼梯导航、坡道、不平整地形）的鲁棒个性化适应，证明了低秩增量学习在可穿戴外骨骼控制中的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Wearable exoskeleton systems hold promise for restoring mobility in individuals with physical impairments, yet most existing controllers rely on static gait policies that lack the ability to adapt to dynamic real-world environments or individual user characteristics. We present \olive (\underline{O}nline \underline{L}ow-rank \underline{I}ncremental Learning for Efficient Adapti\underline{ve} Exoskeletons), a parameter-efficient online adaptation framework that continuously personalizes exoskeleton control during deployment. \olive decomposes the adaptive component of the control policy into a low-rank residual form~$\dW = \At\Bt^\top$ with rank~$r!\ll!\min(d,k)$, reducing online update cost from $\mathcal{O}(dk)$ to $\mathcal{O}(r(d{+}k))$ while preserving the stability of a pretrained base controller~$\Wz$. Parameters are updated via a reward-shaped policy gradient driven purely by on-body sensor feedback (EMG, IMU, vibration), eliminating dependence on offline reference trajectories. A gating mechanism modulates the strength of personalization based on contextual state, and a dynamic rank scheduler adapts the update dimensionality to terrain complexity -- allocating minimal capacity on simple flat terrain and expanding to higher-rank updates on demanding uneven surfaces -- enabling robust performance across diverse activities: flat walking, stair navigation, slopes, and uneven terrain. Experiments on the wearable platform demonstrate that \olive achieves +13, +22, and +15 percentage-point improvements in gait smoothness, effort reduction, and motion stability over the strongest baseline, converging within $\sim$1{,}800 walking steps at 7.4,ms end-to-end latency. Our code implementation is available at https://github.com/FastLM/OLIVE.

</details>


### [125] [Learning Contact Representation for Leg Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05501)
*Emre Girgin,Cagri Kilic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于关节编码器的自监督接触检测框架，无需依赖足端力传感器即可准确识别支撑相与摆动相，在无需额外传感器和标签的情况下实现了优于监督方法和基线概率方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人里程计估计依赖于支撑相期间足部相对于世界静止的假设，而支撑相检测是获取机体速度反馈的关键子问题。尽管许多研究采用足端力传感器，但这些传感器并非所有机器人都具备，且对未考虑的扰动（如滑移）可能不敏感。因此，需要一种依赖标准关节编码器且无需力传感器辅助的鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出一种自监督表示学习框架，仅使用关节编码器数据，无需力传感器增强。通过学习到的表示概率性地建模支撑相与摆动相，实现接触检测。

Result: 实验结果验证了所提自监督接触检测器的有效性。在无需额外传感器和标签的情况下，该框架在性能上优于需要传感器增强和标注的监督方法，以及基线概率方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效，且代码已公开，有助于推广应用于各类足式机器人。

Abstract: The estimation of odometry in legged robots depends on the assumption that the velocity of the foot with respect to the world remains zero during the stance phase. Feedback for the main body velocity is derived from the kinematic serial chain of the feet making accurate leg phase detection is a critical subproblem. A considerable number of studies employ ground reaction force sensors mounted at the tip of the foot to classify, yet these sensors may not be universally available for all legged robots. Additionally, these sensors are often unresponsive to unaccounted disturbances, such as slippage, while the foot remains in contact with the ground. In this study, we propose a self-supervised representation learning framework for contact detection that utilizes the standard sensor set of joint encoders without reliance on force sensor augmentations. We employ learned representations to model the stance and swing phases probabilistically. The experimental results obtained confirm the efficacy of the proposed self-supervised contact detector. Our framework exhibited superior performance in comparison to supervised methods which necessitate sensor set augmentation and labeling, as well as baseline probabilistic approaches. Additionally, we make our code available to the public.

</details>


### [126] [Discrete-WAM: Unified Discrete Vision-Action Token Editing for World-Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05645)
*Ziyang Yao,Haochen Liu,Yuncheng Jiang,Zeyu Zhu,Zibin Guo,Jingru Wang,Tianle Liu,Jianwei Cui,Kuiyuan Yang,Hongwei Xie,Jingwei Zhao,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Discrete-WAM是一个统一的潜在视觉-动作世界策略，通过将未来视觉状态和自我动作表示为对齐的离散标记，建立共享的离散扩散框架来联合建模世界模型、世界-动作策略和分层决策策略，实现跨驾驶场景的组合因果推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要推理自我动作如何塑造周围世界的演化。大多数端到端方法仅依赖状态到动作的直接映射，仅捕获相关性而非显式建模动作条件动力学；而连续潜空间世界模型缺乏跨反事实未来的组合结构以进行因果推理。

Method: 提出Discrete-WAM，一个统一的潜在视觉-动作世界策略，将未来视觉状态和自我动作表示为对齐的离散标记；基于此对齐建立共享的离散扩散框架，联合表述世界建模、世界-动作策略和分层决策策略。

Result: 在大型自动驾驶基准上实现竞争性能，支持可控生成和反事实推理，为更可靠的决策制定提供原则性路径。

Conclusion: Discrete-WAM支持跨多样化驾驶场景的组合泛化，为自动驾驶中更可靠的决策制定提供了一条原则性途径。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires reasoning about how ego actions shape the evolution of the surrounding world. However, most end-to-end methods rely on direct state-to-action mappings, capturing correlations without explicitly modeling action-conditioned dynamics. Conversely, continuous-latent world models often lack compositional structure for causal reasoning across counterfactual futures. We introduce Discrete-WAM, a unified latent vision-action world policy that represents future visual states and ego actions as aligned discrete tokens, enabling compositional causal reasoning across alternative futures. Built upon this unified discrete alignment, Discrete-WAM establishes a shared discrete diffusion framework with unified generative tasks, jointly formulating world modeling, world-action policy, and hierarchical decision-enabled policy, supporting compositional generalization across diverse driving scenarios. Experiments on large-scale autonomous-driving benchmarks show that Discrete-WAM achieves competitive performance while supporting controllable generation and counterfactual reasoning, offering a principled path toward more reliable decision-making.

</details>


### [127] [Visuotactile and Explicitly Force-Controlled Robotic Ultrasound for Abdominal Volumetric Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05848)
*Adrian Piedra,R Brooke Jeffrey,Oussama Khatib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该系统集成立体视觉、触觉反馈和专家策略，通过记录专家放射科医生的运力数据和力数据，实现自主且适应性的腹部超声扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为创建一个能够捕获放射科医生运力、力和扫描策略的框架，使机器人能够执行自主且适应性的腹部扫描。

Method: 记录专家自由手运力与力数据，创建运力、力及解剖扫描策略框架；利用立体视觉生成3D地形图，通过关键点刚度测量细化肋骨边界；采用7自由度柔顺扭矩控制机械臂，通过闭环力控维持探头接触。

Result: 物理实验证明系统能实现与专家相当的高质量成像，并能动态适应患者特定地形；机器人系统超越专家能力，实现3D体积采集。

Conclusion: 该工作突出了专家知识在自主机器人系统中的整合，强调了感知自主性与物理推理相结合在增强诊断性能方面的潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a robotic ultrasound acquisition system that integrates stereo vision, touch-based feedback, and expert-informed strategies to perform autonomous and adaptive abdominal scans. The system records freehand motion and force data from expert radiologists, creating a framework to capture transducer motion, applied forces, and anatomical scanning strategies. This expert data is replayed to replicate characteristic scans with the robot, forming a foundation for further autonomous capabilities. Using stereo vision, the system generates three-dimensional topography maps of the patient's abdomen, which are refined through stiffness measurements at key points to delineate the rib cage boundary. These combined techniques enable the robot to execute two distinct scanning paths: an upward-angled sweep beneath the rib cage to visualize structures near the upper abdomen and a perpendicular sweep across soft tissue regions. A compliant, torque-controlled seven degree-of-freedom robotic manipulator is controlled to maintain consistent probe contact through closed-loop force control over the varied anatomical surfaces. Physical experiments demonstrate that the system achieves high-quality imaging comparable to expert scans while dynamically adapting to patient-specific topographies. Furthermore, the robotic system surpasses expert capabilities by enabling three-dimensional volume acquisition, which enhances diagnostic potential and provides volumetric data for advanced analyses. This work highlights the integration of expert knowledge into autonomous robotic systems and underscores the potential of combining perception-based autonomy with physical reasoning for enhanced diagnostic performance.

</details>


### [128] [Towards a Data Flywheel for Embodied Intelligence in Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05960)
*Anlan Yu,Zaishu Chen,Zhiqing Hong,Daqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种面向物流行业的具身智能数据中心化框架（物流数据飞轮），通过将日常运营转化为可复用数据资产，利用世界模型为长尾分拣任务提供监督信号，并将部署反馈用于策略改进。核心成果是WM-DAgger世界模型数据聚合框架，用于合成分布外恢复数据以增强模仿学习的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能正从实验室演示走向工业部署，物流行业是关键应用场景。基于学习的策略为超越传统感知-规划-控制流水线提供了有前景的路径，但其可扩展性取决于如何采集、组织与复用具身数据。

Method: 提出数据中心化的工业具身智能框架，构建“物流数据飞轮”：1）将日常运营转化为可复用数据资产；2）使用世界模型为长尾分拣任务生成可靠监督信号；3）将部署反馈回流至策略改进。

Result: 提出WM-DAgger世界模型数据聚合框架，合成分布外恢复数据以实现鲁棒模仿学习。后续工作探索大规模野外多模态数据（标注演示、未标注视频、机器人日志）的对齐与策略学习，并转化为持续系统改进的反馈。

Conclusion: 该框架通过闭环数据飞轮解决具身智能的数据可扩展性问题，将日常运营数据转化为可复用资产，结合世界模型与持续反馈实现策略优化与部署闭环。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is moving from laboratory demonstrations toward industrial deployment, with the logistics industry serving as a key application scenario. Learning-based policies offer a promising path beyond traditional perception-planning-control pipelines, but their scalability depends on how embodied data can be collected, organized, and reused. This research studies a data-centric framework for industrial embodied intelligence by constructing a logistics data flywheel. Our framework converts daily operations into reusable data assets, uses World Models to generate reliable supervision for long-tail parcel manipulation, and feeds deployment feedback back into policy improvement. As an initial result, \textit{WM-DAgger} introduces a World-Model-based data aggregation framework that synthesizes out-of-distribution recovery data for robust imitation learning. Building on this result, ongoing work explores how large-scale in-the-wild multimodal data, including labeled human demonstrations, unlabeled operational videos, and system-level robot logs, can be aligned for policy learning and transformed into feedback for continual system improvement.

</details>


### [129] [A Conversational Framework for Human-Robot Collaborative Manipulation with Distributed Generative AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06061)
*Arash Ghasemzadeh Kakroudi,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种分布式对话框架，将本地语言和视觉语言模型与ROS 2执行栈集成，用于人机协作操作。系统将语言理解、视觉定位、编排和运动执行作为独立的ROS 2节点运行，实现灵活的分布式硬件部署和响应式控制循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决人机协作操作中硬件部署灵活性和控制循环响应性的问题，提出一种分布式对话框架，使系统能够在分布式硬件上运行，同时保持响应式控制。

Method: 将语言理解、视觉定位、编排和运动执行作为独立的ROS 2节点实现，接收自然语言指令，通过VLM生成图像空间目标，使用深度和标定转换为机器人坐标系下的目标，通过Web仪表板展示中间意图和定位叠加信息，并在执行任何运动前要求操作员确认。

Result: 在Franka FR3平台上进行了实验，评估了端到端任务可靠性和延迟，测试了在不同工作场景模糊度增加条件下的性能，并比较了管道中不同的LLM/VLM配置。

Conclusion: 该分布式对话框架有效支持人机协作操作，代码和完整文档已在GitHub开源（https://github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm），证明了在分布式硬件上部署复杂机器人系统的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed conversational framework for human-robot collaborative manipulation that integrates local language and vision-language models (VLMs) with a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2)-based execution stack. Language understanding, visual grounding, orchestration, and motion execution run as separate ROS 2 nodes, enabling flexible deployment across distributed hardware while maintaining a responsive control loop. From free-form user commands, the system generates structured action requests for pick, place, and handover. It uses a VLM to return image-space targets, which are converted into metric robot-frame goals using depth and calibration. A web dashboard exposes intermediate intent and grounding overlays (pixel, depth, and robot-frame) and requires explicit operator confirmation before any motion is executed. Experiments on a Franka FR3 platform evaluate end-to-end task reliability and latency under increasing working table scene ambiguity and compare alternative LLM/VLM configurations in the same pipeline. Code and full documentation are available at [github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm](https://github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm).

</details>


### [130] [CLEAR: Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06219)
*Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Zhiyuan Liu,Yanbo Jiang,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLEAR是一个端到端自动驾驶框架，通过单步条件漂移和语义推理实现高效多模态规划，在NAVSIM v1基准上达到93.7的PDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型难以平衡多模态机动生成与实时推理约束；扩散模型虽能捕获多样化驾驶行为，但其迭代去噪过程带来不可接受的延迟，不满足安全关键部署要求。

Method: CLEAR框架包含三个核心组件：(1)使用Drive-JEPA作为视觉编码器；(2)将扩散模型的去噪链替换为VAE潜在空间中的单步条件漂移，引入条件系数α平衡多样性与专家精度；(3)微调Qwen3.5-0.8B处理驾驶问答数据提取场景感知隐藏状态，通过自适应调度器选择α和样本数N，以及交叉注意力评分器筛选最优轨迹。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1基准上达到93.7的PDMS（规划密度与机动成功率评分），实现了无需密集几何标注或迭代采样的高效高保真多模态规划。

Conclusion: 证明了在安全关键场景中，无需迭代采样和密集标注即可实现高效、高保真的多模态规划，平衡了生成多样性与实时推理需求。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models often struggle to balance multi-modal maneuver generation with real-time inference constraints. While diffusion models successfully capture diverse driving behaviors, their iterative denoising process incurs unacceptable latency for safety-critical deployment. To address this, we propose CLEAR (Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing), a framework that combines ultra-fast generative planning with deep semantic reasoning. CLEAR employs Drive-JEPA as the visual encoder and replaces the multi-step denoising chain with a single-step conditional drift in a VAE latent space, introducing a conditioning coefficient to balance diversity and expert precision. Meanwhile, we fully fine-tune Qwen~3.5~0.8B on driving QA pairs to extract scene-aware hidden states. These states guide both an Adaptive Scheduler, which selects the conditioning coefficient $α$ and sample count $N$ from a discrete set of predefined schemes, and a cross-attention scorer that selects the optimal trajectory from candidates. On the NAVSIM v1 benchmark, CLEAR achieves a state-of-the-art PDMS of 93.7. Our results demonstrate that high-fidelity, multi-modal planning can be executed efficiently without dense geometric annotations or iterative sampling.

</details>


### [131] [MPCoT: Reward-Guided Multi-Path Latent Reasoning for Test-Time Scalable Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06245)
*Boyang Zhang,Lianlei Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MPCoT是一种奖励引导的多路径潜在推理框架，通过在动作解码前初始化和细化多个假设路径，在保持原有动作接口的同时提升VLA策略在长时域和高不确定性控制中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略在长时域和高不确定性控制中表现脆弱，单次动作解码提供有限的推理时间推演能力；显式链式推理虽能增加推理深度，但引入了令牌延迟和间接的文本到动作接口。

Method: MPCoT初始化M个假设，经过K步权重共享的细化，在动作解码前进行软聚合。训练阶段采用路径偏好目标，通过专家动作一致性、世界模型/VLM驱动的进展和成功反馈来评估候选动作分支，使潜在路径评分器与下游执行质量对齐。

Result: 在LIBERO和CALVIN任务上采用匹配协议，MPCoT显著提升了长时域性能。消融实验证实了深度-宽度效应、置信度加权聚合以及奖励引导的路径监督的有效性。

Conclusion: MPCoT在不生成推理令牌的情况下实现了深度推理，保持了原始8步动作接口，并提供了可配置的推理控制参数(K, M)，有效解决了VLA策略在复杂控制任务中的脆弱性问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies remain brittle in long-horizon and high-uncertainty control, where one-pass action decoding provides limited inference-time deliberation. Explicit chain-of-thought can increase reasoning depth, but introduces token latency and an indirect text-to-action interface. We propose MPCoT, a reward-guided multi-path latent reasoning framework that initializes $M$ hypotheses, refines them for K weight-tied steps, and softly aggregates them before action decoding. A training-only path-preference objective evaluates candidate action branches with expert-action consistency, world-model/VLM-based progress, and success feedback to align the latent path scorer with downstream execution quality. MPCoT preserves the original 8-step action interface, generates zero reasoning tokens, and exposes configurable inference controls (K,M). Under matched protocols on LIBERO and CALVIN, MPCoT improves long-horizon performance, with ablations confirming depth-width effects, confidence-weighted aggregation, and reward-guided path supervision.

</details>


### [132] [RadiusFPS: Efficient Farthest Point Sampling on CPUs and GPUs via Spherical Voxel Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06255)
*Ziyang Yu,Xiang Li,Qiong Chang,Jun Miyazaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RadiusFPS是一种基于球形体素修剪的FPS加速框架，通过GPU优化实现2.5倍加速，在保持精度的同时大幅降低内存使用，使高质量FPS采样适用于实时机器人视觉系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FPS采样器时间复杂度高，无法满足现代3D传感器每秒百万点率的实时要求，成为延迟瓶颈，与机器人系统的实时性和有限计算预算冲突。

Method: 基于球形体素修剪的FPS加速框架，保持标准FPS更新规则和初始化/打破平局策略；通过球形体素索引推导保守几何边界修剪冗余距离计算；坐标-wise点跳过测试去除残差更新；RadiusFPS-G采用warp-level GPU实现，融合体素选择、修剪和距离更新为内存合并内核。

Result: 在室内(S3DIS, ScanNet)和室外LiDAR(SemanticKITTI)基准测试中，RadiusFPS-G相比GPU FPS提升2.5倍；与QuickFPS相当或更好且内存使用约为一半；分割精度保持相当；与FastPoint采样器结合后实现最快的端到端推理。

Conclusion: 高质量FPS风格采样在延迟和内存受限的机器人视觉中变得实用，为LiDAR自动驾驶、SLAM和导航等应用提供实时高效解决方案。

Abstract: Point clouds are a primary sensory representation for robotic perception, underpinning LiDAR-based autonomous driving, simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM), and navigation. Within these pipelines, Farthest Point Sampling (FPS) is the most well-known downsampling operator, as its uniform coverage preserves the geometric structure on which downstream perception relies. However, the large time complexity of classical FPS scales poorly with the million-point-per-second rates of modern 3D sensors, making it a dominant latency bottleneck that conflicts with the real-time and limited onboard compute budgets of robotic systems. Therefore, we propose RadiusFPS, an FPS acceleration framework based on spherical voxel pruning that preserves the standard FPS update rule under the same initialization and tie-breaking policy. By indexing the point cloud with spherical voxels, RadiusFPS derives a conservative geometric bound that prunes redundant distance computations in each iteration, complemented by a coordinate-wise point-skip test that removes residual updates. We further introduce RadiusFPS-G, a warp-level GPU implementation that fuses voxel selection, pruning, and distance update into memory-coalesced kernels, eliminating costly global-memory round-trips. On indoor (S3DIS, ScanNet) and outdoor LiDAR (SemanticKITTI) benchmarks, RadiusFPS-G attains up to 2.5x speedup over GPU-based FPS and matches or exceeds QuickFPS among the evaluated methods while using roughly half its GPU memory, with comparable segmentation accuracy. When coupled with the learning-based FastPoint sampler, the resulting pipeline achieves the fastest End-to-End inference among all evaluated configurations. These properties make high-quality FPS-style sampling practical for latency- and memory-constrained robotic vision.

</details>


### [133] [Meridian: Metric-Semantic Primitive Matching for Cross-View Geo-Localization Beyond Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06312)
*Mason Peterson,Qingyuan Li,Yixuan Jia,Fernando Cladera,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Camillo Jose Taylor,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Meridian是一种跨航空图像与地面机器人RGB-D数据的匹配方法，通过高层度量和语义原语实现GNSS拒止环境下的准确全局定位，无需针对特定区域训练或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自动化需要精确的全局定位来支持可重复性、任务规划、目标指定和安全操作。现有基于航空影像的方法主要针对结构化城市环境，在非结构化自然地形中很少得到验证，且存在对特定环境训练模型的依赖、难以处理重复几何和特征稀疏景观等局限性。

Method: 提出Meridian方法，在高层度量和语义原语层面匹配航空图像与地面机器人RGB-D数据，实现无需特定区域训练或微调的跨环境泛化定位。通过新颖的一致性度量估计机器人子地图位姿分布，并在鲁棒位姿图优化步骤中拒绝异常假设，从而获得准确的机器人轨迹估计。

Result: 在自动驾驶数据集、公园校园区域和野外营地等多种环境中成功定位地面机器人，19公里地面行程平均优化轨迹误差为2.4米。

Conclusion: Meridian在无需区域特定训练的情况下实现了跨多种环境的准确全局定位，在结构化和非结构化环境中均表现良好，验证了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Successful robot automation requires accurate global localization to support repeatability, task planning, goal specification, and safe operation. However, reliable localization in GNSS-denied environments remains an open problem. Overhead aerial imagery offers a promising solution, but existing approaches primarily target structured urban environments and have been rarely demonstrated in unstructured natural terrain. Limitations of the state-of-the-art include a reliance on models trained for specific environments, as well as difficulty handling repetitive geometries and featureless landscapes commonly found in natural outdoor areas. To overcome these challenges, we present Meridian, a method for matching high-level metric-semantic primitives across aerial images and ground robot RGB-D camera data that achieves accurate global localization and generalizes well across diverse environments, all without any training or algorithmic fine-tuning on area-specific data. We formulate novel consistency metrics to estimate a distribution over robot submap poses and to reject outlier hypotheses in a robust pose graph optimization step for accurate robot trajectory estimation. We demonstrate that our algorithm can localize a ground robot across a wide variety of environments, including an autonomous driving dataset, a park and campus area, and a wilderness camp, with an average optimized trajectory error of 2.4 m over 19 km of ground traversal.

</details>


### [134] [Waypoints Matter: A Systematic Study for Sampling-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06366)
*Josep M. Barbera,Antonio Artuñedo,Jorge Villagra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究将航点放置作为设计变量，对比三种策略在449种配置和5个CommonRoad地图上的性能，发现均匀间隔是主要调优参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时自动驾驶轨迹规划中，航点放置直接影响可行轨迹的存在性和质量，但其对规划器性能的影响尚未被充分探索，需要系统研究航点放置策略对规划器性能的影响。

Method: 将航点放置作为一等设计变量，固定轨迹原语和候选预算，系统性地对比三种放置策略：1)均匀间隔采样；2)增强版Ramer-Douglas-Peucker变体(RDP*)；3)新的曲率条件分配策略。在449种配置和5个CommonRoad地图（几何复杂度递增）上进行实验。

Result: 标称航点间隔ds是主要性能驱动因素，仅靠放置方式就导致规划器可靠性有显著差异。在调优间隔下的均匀采样在可靠性和质量上均匹配或超过RDP*和中心曲率变体。曲率变体在几何复杂道路的可靠性优先和平衡权重设置下有微小但一致的提升，而RDP*从未优于均匀采样。

Conclusion: ds应作为主要调优参数，曲率感知策略适用于曲率丰富的走廊，当可行性是限制因素时使用。这意味着在大多数情况下，简单的均匀间隔采样配合适当的间距调优就足够了，复杂策略仅在特定几何条件下提供微小优势。

Abstract: Real-time autonomous driving commonly relies on sampling-based trajectory planners that link candidate trajectories to target waypoints along the road centerline. The placement of these waypoints directly impacts both the existence and quality of feasible trajectories. Yet, its effect on planner performance remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we treat waypoint placement as a first-class design variable. We hold the trajectory primitive and candidate budget fixed, and systematically sweep three placement strategies (uniform spacing, an augmented Ramer-Douglas-Peucker variant (RDP*), and a novel curvature-conditioned allocation) across 449 configurations and five CommonRoad maps of increasing geometric complexity. Our results show that the nominal inter-waypoint spacing $d_s$ is the primary performance driver, with large differences in planner reliability attributed to placement alone. Uniform sampling at a well-tuned spacing matches or surpasses both RDP* and the centered curvature variant. The curvature variant offers a small but consistent advantage on geometrically complex roads under reliability-first and balanced weightings, while RDP* never outperforms uniform sampling. These findings suggest that $d_s$ should be treated as the dominant tuning parameter, with geometry-aware strategies reserved for curvature-rich corridors where feasibility is the limiting factor.

</details>


### [135] [RiskFlow: Fast and Faithful Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06423)
*Qi Lan,Yining Tang,Yu Shen,Yi Zhou,Yuhao Wei,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RiskFlow是一种用于生成安全关键交通场景的闭环多智能体交通生成框架，通过在动作空间中的输运来预测未来轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法计算成本高，迭代去噪过程容易在长时间滚动中累积采样和引导误差，导致抖动、异常加速和偏离道路等不真实的运动伪影。

Method: RiskFlow将未来轨迹生成建模为动作空间中的输运，学习有限时间间隔内的平均速度场，利用JVP（雅可比向量积）目标函数进行高效稳定的训练；测试时采用输出空间引导来引导关键智能体进入危险交互，同时规范偏离道路行为，并通过车辆动力学重建物理可行轨迹。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集的tbsim闭环评估中，RiskFlow实现了强的对抗性-真实感权衡，在多智能体和长时间预测场景下提升真实感的同时保持竞争力的安全关键生成能力，并显著减少推理时间。

Conclusion: RiskFlow有效解决了现有扩散方法在安全关键交通场景生成中计算开销大、误差累积导致的运动伪影问题，在保持生成安全性的同时提高了场景真实性和评估效率。

Abstract: Safety-critical traffic scenario generation is essential for evaluating autonomous driving systems under rare but high-risk interactions. Existing diffusion-based methods offer strong controllability in closed-loop generation, but their iterative denoising process is computationally expensive and may accumulate sampling and guidance errors over long rollouts, causing unrealistic motion artifacts such as jitter, abnormal acceleration, and off-road behavior. To address these issues, we propose RiskFlow, a closed-loop safety-critical multi-agent traffic generation framework that formulates future trajectory generation as transport in the action space. Instead of relying on iterative denoising, RiskFlow learns an average velocity field over a finite interval to transform Gaussian action sequences into future acceleration and yaw-rate commands with a single forward pass, using a JVP-based objective for efficient and stable training. At test time, RiskFlow applies output-space guidance to the generated actions, steering selected critical agents toward risky interactions while regularizing off-road behavior, and reconstructs physically feasible trajectories through vehicle dynamics. Experiments on nuScenes with tbsim closed-loop evaluation show that RiskFlow achieves a strong adversariality-realism trade-off across multi-agent and long-horizon settings. Compared with representative baselines, RiskFlow consistently improves realism while maintaining competitive safety-critical generation capability, and substantially reduces inference time for evaluation.

</details>


### [136] [Ask-to-Clarify: Resolving Instruction Ambiguity through Multi-turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15061)
*Xingyao Lin,Xinghao Zhu,Tianyi Lu,Sicheng Xie,Hui Zhang,Xipeng Qiu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Ask-to-Clarify框架，通过多轮对话解决指令歧义问题，结合VLM与扩散模型实现协作式具身智能体，在8个真实任务中超越现有SOTA VLA


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA具身智能体多为单向执行模式，缺乏与人类反馈的交互能力，无法处理真实场景中常见的歧义指令，难以成为真正的协作者而非被动执行器

Method: 提出Ask-to-Clarify框架：包含协作型VLM、扩散动作模型和连接模块；采用两阶段知识隔离训练策略（先微调VLM处理歧义，再冻结VLM微调扩散模型生成动作）；推理时使用信号检测器作为路由器在提问和行动间切换

Result: 在8个真实世界任务中评估，该框架显著超越现有SOTA VLA，证明了其作为迈向协作式具身智能体可行路径的有效性

Conclusion: 通过Ask-to-Clarify框架和两阶段训练策略，实现了具身智能体从单向执行到主动澄清歧义、与人类协作的转变，为构建真正的协作式具身智能体提供了可行路径

Abstract: The ultimate goal of embodied agents is to create collaborators that can interact with humans, not mere executors that passively follow instructions. This requires agents to communicate, coordinate, and adapt their actions based on human feedback. Recently, advances in VLAs have offered a path toward this goal. However, most current VLA-based embodied agents operate in a one-way mode: they receive an instruction and execute it without feedback. This approach fails in real-world scenarios where instructions are often ambiguous. In this paper, we address this problem with the Ask-to-Clarify framework. Our framework first resolves ambiguous instructions by asking questions in a multi-turn dialogue. Then it generates low-level actions end-to-end. Specifically, the Ask-to-Clarify framework consists of two components, one VLM for collaboration and one diffusion for action. We also introduce a connection module that generates conditions for the diffusion based on the output of the VLM. This module adjusts the observation by instructions to create reliable conditions. We train our framework with a two-stage knowledge-insulation strategy. First, we fine-tune the collaboration component using ambiguity-solving dialogue data to handle ambiguity. Then, we integrate the action component while freezing the collaboration one. This preserves the interaction abilities while fine-tuning the diffusion to generate actions. The training strategy guarantees our framework can first ask questions, then generate actions. During inference, a signal detector functions as a router that helps our framework switch between asking questions and taking actions. We evaluate the Ask-to-Clarify framework in 8 real-world tasks, where it outperforms existing state-of-the-art VLAs. The results suggest that our proposed framework, along with the training strategy, provides a path toward collaborative embodied agents.

</details>
